Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the chemical framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 577. The resulting operator achieves bound O(763/n^1).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the chemical decomposition. By minimality, the element a^577 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 763-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
